Tier B — needs Nick (145)Tier A — mechanical (1)Tier C — deliberate (6)PagesSource scan

How to read this

Each finding is one part of the app (for example the page title on workspace pages at 1512px wide) that is drawn more than one way within a family of pages. Pages are compared only within their family (workspace, non-workspace, onboarding, public, overlays); the Dashboard joins the workspace pages for buttons, cards, text and form controls only. Variant 1 is the most common, which is evidence, not the answer: a variant is marked "oracle" only where a shared token settles it.

Hard-coded colours that equal an app-wide token (6835 literals in 408 files)

Each of these literals is exactly the value of an app-wide token (index.css, the shell's stylesheets or designTokens.ts). They render identically today, so none shows as a difference on screen. They are listed as candidates for a sweep, not fixed here: a value that equals a token is not always that token (most #ffffff is not "the shell's active fill"), so each swap needs a reading of what the colour is for.
